- **dokploy-internal-oneshot**: Use when running an ephemeral one-shot task (data migration, batch job, schema setup, ad-hoc psql script) that must reach other services in a Dokploy project's internal network without exposing those services to the public internet. The task should join the project's docker overlay network, run to completion, and exit. Pairs with dokploy-api-cli. ([Innei/SKILL](https://github.com/Innei/SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/infrastructure/dokploy-internal-oneshot) · ★81)
- **dokploy-mcp**: AI-driven deployment management using Dokploy MCP server for projects, applications, and databases ([enuno/claude-command-and-control](https://github.com/enuno/claude-command-and-control/tree/HEAD/skills-templates/dokploy-mcp) · ★15)
- **dokploy-traefik-traffic-split**: Use when you need to canary-deploy two HTTP backends on the same Dokploy-managed domain with a percentage split (e.g. migrating an SPA from one framework to another). Covers the critical SPA-asset trap that breaks naive sticky-cookie splits, a path-aware Traefik config that solves it, and a four-phase workflow (backup → dry-run on a canary host → roll to prod → quick rollback). ([Innei/SKILL](https://github.com/Innei/SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/infrastructure/dokploy-traefik-traffic-split) · ★81)
